110+  67C3 C9                   RET

  6+  67D5
  7+  67D5              FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT EQU #FFFF
  8+  67D5              IPC_BYTE_TIMEOUT EQU #FFFF
  9+  67D5              IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT EQU #FFFF
 10+  67D5
 11+  67D5              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 12+  67D5              ; Open the single NeoGS package on TR-DOS and read its first sector to #C000.
 13+  67D5              ; The same 256-byte buffer is reused for every following sector while the
 14+  67D5              ; firmware is streamed to NeoGS. The complete file is NEVER staged in host RAM.
 15+  67D5              ; OUT: Carry clear, first sector at #C000, file length and next-sector cursor set.
 16+  67D5              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 17+  67D5              load_neogs_firmware_file:
 17+  67D5
 18+  67D5                      ; Search the exact 8.3 catalogue name neoprog.C.
 19+  67D5 21 9C 6B             ld      hl, neo_fw_filename
 20+  67D8 11 DD 5C             ld      de, #5CDD
 21+  67DB 01 08 00             ld      bc, 8
 22+  67DE ED B0                ldir
 23+  67E0 3E 43                ld      a, 'C'
 24+  67E2 32 E5 5C             ld      (#5CE5), a
 25+  67E5 3E 09                ld      a, 9
 26+  67E7 32 06 5D             ld      (#5D06), a
 27+  67EA 0E 0A                ld      c, 10
 28+  67EC CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
 29+  67EF 79                   ld      a, c
 30+  67F0 FE FF                cp      #FF
 31+  67F2 CA 57 68             jp      z, .file_not_found
 32+  67F5
 33+  67F5                      ; C=#0A returns the directory slot. Fetch its full descriptor with C=#08.
 34+  67F5 F5                   push    af
 35+  67F6 AF                   xor     a
 36+  67F7 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
 37+  67FA 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
 38+  67FD F1                   pop     af
 39+  67FE 0E 08                ld      c, 8
 40+  6800 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
 41+  6803 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
 42+  6806 47                   ld      b, a
 43+  6807 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
 44+  680A B0                   or      b
 45+  680B C2 5B 68             jp      nz, .bad_descriptor
 46+  680E
 47+  680E                      ; Store the actual package length; its header separates the two code sections.
 48+  680E 2A E8 5C             ld      hl, (#5CE8)
 49+  6811 22 AC 6B             ld      (neo_fw_file_length), hl
 50+  6814 7C                   ld      a, h
 51+  6815 B5                   or      l
 52+  6816 CA 5F 68             jp      z, .bad_length
 53+  6819 7C                   ld      a, h
 54+  681A B7                   or      a
 55+  681B 20 06                jr      nz, .package_minimum_ok
 56+  681D 7D                   ld      a, l
 57+  681E FE 03                cp      3
 58+  6820 DA 5F 68             jp      c, .bad_length
 59+  6823              .package_minimum_ok:
 60+  6823                      ; Verify sector count = ceil(file_length / 256).
 61+  6823 2A AC 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_file_length)
 62+  6826 11 FF 00             ld      de, 255
 63+  6829 19                   add     hl, de
 64+  682A 3A EA 5C             ld      a, (#5CEA)
 65+  682D BC                   cp      h
 66+  682E C2 5B 68             jp      nz, .bad_descriptor
 67+  6831 B7                   or      a
 68+  6832 CA 5B 68             jp      z, .bad_descriptor
 69+  6835 32 A6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a
 70+  6838
 71+  6838 3A EB 5C             ld      a, (#5CEB)
 72+  683B FE 10                cp      16
 73+  683D D2 5B 68             jp      nc, .bad_descriptor
 74+  6840 32 AA 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
 75+  6843 3A EC 5C             ld      a, (#5CEC)
 76+  6846 32 A9 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_track), a
 77+  6849 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
 78+  684C 22 A4 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_buffer), hl
 79+  684F
 80+  684F                      ; Read only the first sector now. Later sectors are fetched on demand
 81+  684F                      ; by upload_firmware after the preceding buffer bytes have been sent.
 82+  684F CD 6A 68             call    neo_fw_read_next_sector
 83+  6852 DA 63 68             jp      c, .sector_read_failed
 84+  6855 AF                   xor     a
 85+  6856 C9                   ret
 86+  6857
 87+  6857              .file_not_found:
 88+  6857 3E 01                ld      a, 1
 89+  6859 18 0A                jr      .load_failed
 90+  685B              .bad_descriptor:
 91+  685B 3E 02                ld      a, 2
 92+  685D 18 06                jr      .load_failed
 93+  685F              .bad_length:
 94+  685F 3E 03                ld      a, 3
 95+  6861 18 02                jr      .load_failed
 96+  6863              .sector_read_failed:
 97+  6863 3E 04                ld      a, 4
 98+  6865              .load_failed:
 99+  6865 32 AB 6B             ld      (neo_fw_file_error), a
100+  6868 37                   scf
101+  6869 C9                   ret
102+  686A
103+  686A              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
104+  686A              ; Read one TR-DOS sector into the reusable #C000 buffer.
105+  686A              ; Uses neo_fw_load_track/sector/remaining. Advances the cursor only after a
106+  686A              ; successful read. OUT: carry clear; carry set on disk error/exhaustion.
107+  686A              ; Clobbers AF, BC, DE, HL.
108+  686A              neo_fw_read_next_sector:
109+  686A 3A A6 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_remaining)
110+  686D B7                   or      a
111+  686E 20 02                jr      nz, .sector_available
112+  6870 37                   scf
113+  6871 C9                   ret
114+  6872              .sector_available:
115+  6872 3A A9 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
116+  6875 57                   ld      d, a
117+  6876 3A AA 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
118+  6879 5F                   ld      e, a
119+  687A 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
120+  687D 06 01                ld      b, 1
121+  687F 0E 05                ld      c, 5
122+  6881 AF                   xor     a
123+  6882 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
124+  6885 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
125+  6888 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
126+  688B 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
127+  688E 47                   ld      b, a
128+  688F 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
129+  6892 B0                   or      b
130+  6893 20 21                jr      nz, .sector_io_error
131+  6895
132+  6895                      ; Advance TR-DOS cursor: sectors 0..15, then next track.
133+  6895 3A AA 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_sector)
134+  6898 3C                   inc     a
135+  6899 FE 10                cp      16
136+  689B 38 0D                jr      c, .sector_same_track
137+  689D AF                   xor     a
138+  689E 32 AA 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
139+  68A1 3A A9 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_track)
140+  68A4 3C                   inc     a
141+  68A5 32 A9 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_track), a
142+  68A8 18 03                jr      .sector_cursor_advanced
143+  68AA              .sector_same_track:
144+  68AA 32 AA 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_sector), a
145+  68AD              .sector_cursor_advanced:
146+  68AD 3A A6 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_load_remaining)
147+  68B0 3D                   dec     a
148+  68B1 32 A6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_load_remaining), a
149+  68B4 B7                   or      a
150+  68B5 C9                   ret
151+  68B6              .sector_io_error:
152+  68B6 37                   scf
153+  68B7 C9                   ret
154+  68B8
155+  68B8              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
156+  68B8              ; upload_firmware
157+  68B8              ; IN : HL = #C000 reusable first-sector buffer, DE = total package length.
158+  68B8              ; OUT: Carry clear only after custom firmware sends NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK.
159+  68B8              ;
160+  68B8              ; Cold/warm reset path:
161+  68B8              ;   #F4 cold restart
162+  68B8              ;   #20 get total RAM, read three bytes to prove stock ROM is ready
163+  68B8              ;   #14 receive executable bootstrap directly into RAM at $4000
164+  68B8              ;   #13 jump to $4000
165+  68B8              ;   #A1 bootstrap ready, send length, receive #A3 length accepted
166+  68B8              ;   send custom firmware bytes to RAM $0000, receive #A2 firmware ready
167+  68B8              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
168+  68B8              upload_firmware:
168+  68B8
169+  68B8                      ; Only the first sector is resident at #C000. Remaining sectors are
170+  68B8                      ; read into this same buffer as the firmware stream consumes them.
171+  68B8                      ; Layout: WORD bootstrap_length, bootstrap bytes, then firmware bytes.
172+  68B8 22 AE 6B             ld      (neo_fw_package_source), hl
173+  68BB ED 53 B0 6B          ld      (neo_fw_package_length), de
174+  68BF
175+  68BF                      ; Decode and validate the bootstrap length from the package header.
176+  68BF 7A                   ld      a, d
177+  68C0 B7                   or      a
178+  68C1 20 06                jr      nz, .package_header_present
179+  68C3 7B                   ld      a, e
180+  68C4 FE 03                cp      3
181+  68C6 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
182+  68C9              .package_header_present:
183+  68C9 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
184+  68CA 32 B4 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length), a
185+  68CD 23                   inc     hl
186+  68CE 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
187+  68CF 32 B5 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_length+1), a
188+  68D2 2A B4 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
189+  68D5 7C                   ld      a, h
190+  68D6 B5                   or      l
191+  68D7 CA 83 6A             jp      z, .upload_failed
192+  68DA                      ; Bootstrap must fit entirely in the first 256-byte sector because it
193+  68DA                      ; is needed before the receiver can be started. The package itself may
194+  68DA                      ; be much larger; only this first sector is buffered at a time.
195+  68DA 7C                   ld      a, h
196+  68DB B7                   or      a
197+  68DC C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
198+  68DF 7D                   ld      a, l
199+  68E0 FE FF                cp      255                   ; maximum length 254 bytes
200+  68E2 D2 83 6A             jp      nc, .upload_failed
201+  68E5
202+  68E5                      ; Firmware length = package length - header - bootstrap length.
203+  68E5 2A B0 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_length)
204+  68E8 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
205+  68EB B7                   or      a
206+  68EC ED 52                sbc     hl, de
207+  68EE DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
208+  68F1 ED 5B B4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
209+  68F5 B7                   or      a
210+  68F6 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
211+  68F8 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
212+  68FB 22 B8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_length), hl
213+  68FE
214+  68FE                      ; Bootstrap and the beginning of firmware follow the two-byte header
215+  68FE                      ; in the first sector. The firmware pointer is advanced as bytes go.
216+  68FE 2A AE 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_package_source)
217+  6901 23                   inc     hl
218+  6902 23                   inc     hl
219+  6903 22 B2 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bootstrap_source), hl
220+  6906 ED 5B B4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
221+  690A 19                   add     hl, de
222+  690B 22 B6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
223+  690E
224+  690E                      ; Number of firmware bytes remaining in the first sector:
225+  690E                      ; 256 - (2-byte header + bootstrap length) = 254 - bootstrap length.
226+  690E 21 FE 00             ld      hl, 254
227+  6911 B7                   or      a
228+  6912 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
229+  6914 22 A7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
230+  6917
231+  6917                      ; V1 resident firmware is still limited to WIN0. During transfer the bootstrap
232+  6917                      ; stack remains at #FFFE in WIN3; the resident entry later moves its stack
233+  6917                      ; to WIN1/#4FFE, leaving WIN2 available for the future banked-image format.
234+  6917 2A B8 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
235+  691A 7C                   ld      a, h
236+  691B B7                   or      a
237+  691C C2 26 69             jp      nz, .firmware_high_nonzero
238+  691F 7D                   ld      a, l
239+  6920 B7                   or      a
240+  6921 CA 83 6A             jp      z, .upload_failed
241+  6924 18 0D                jr      .firmware_length_valid
242+  6926              .firmware_high_nonzero:
243+  6926 7C                   ld      a, h
244+  6927 FE 40                cp      #40
245+  6929 38 08                jr      c, .firmware_length_valid
246+  692B C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
247+  692E 7D                   ld      a, l
248+  692F B7                   or      a
249+  6930 C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
250+  6933              .firmware_length_valid:
251+  6933 AF                   xor     a
252+  6934 32 C4 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), a
253+  6937 32 C5 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent+1), a
254+  693A 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
255+  693D DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
256+  693F 32 C6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_initial_status), a
257+  6942 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
258+  6945
259+  6945                      ; Phase 1: documented GS105a cold restart. Also returns a running
260+  6945                      ; Project 124 firmware to the stock ROM before the new bootstrap.
261+  6945 3E 01                ld      a, 1
262+  6947 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
263+  694A 3E F4                ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
264+  694C CD AA 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
265+  694F DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
266+  6952
267+  6952                      ; Phase 2: #20 returns three RAM-size bytes only after stock ROM is up.
268+  6952                      ; Retry #F4 once: if the previous run was interrupted during DMA, the
269+  6952                      ; first #F4 may have been consumed as the DMA end marker; the second
270+  6952                      ; reaches the command dispatcher after it returns to its main loop.
271+  6952 3E 02                ld      a, 2
272+  6954 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
273+  6957 CD 85 6A             call    gs_boot_probe_rom
274+  695A 30 18                jr      nc, .stock_rom_ready
275+  695C 3E 01                ld      a, 1
276+  695E 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
277+  6961 3E F4                ld      a, GS_CMD_COLD_RESTART
278+  6963 CD AA 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
279+  6966 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
280+  6969 3E 02                ld      a, 2
281+  696B 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
282+  696E CD 85 6A             call    gs_boot_probe_rom
283+  6971 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
284+  6974              .stock_rom_ready:
285+  6974
286+  6974                      ; Phase 3: use the native GS105a memory loader, not the MOD player.
287+  6974                      ; #14 RECEIVE BLOCK expects the first (negative length low) byte
288+  6974                      ; before the command, then negative length high, address low/high,
289+  6974                      ; and exactly N payload bytes. The payload is written directly to RAM.
290+  6974 3E 03                ld      a, 3
291+  6976 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
292+  6979 21 00 00             ld      hl, 0
293+  697C ED 5B B4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
294+  6980 B7                   or      a
295+  6981 ED 52                sbc     hl, de                  ; HL = -bootstrap length
296+  6983 22 BC 6B             ld      (neo_fw_block_length), hl
297+  6986 7D                   ld      a, l
298+  6987 CD EC 6A             call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
299+  698A DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
300+  698D 3E 14                ld      a, GS_CMD_RECEIVE_BLOCK
301+  698F CD CF 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command
302+  6992 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
303+  6995
304+  6995                      ; Remaining #14 header: -length high, destination $4000.
305+  6995 3A BD 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_block_length+1)
306+  6998 CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
307+  699B DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
308+  699E AF                   xor     a
309+  699F CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination low = $00
310+  69A2 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
311+  69A5 3E 40                ld      a, #40
312+  69A7 CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte  ; destination high = $40
313+  69AA DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
314+  69AD
315+  69AD                      ; Phase 4: transfer the executable bootstrap into RAM $4000.
316+  69AD 3E 04                ld      a, 4
317+  69AF 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
318+  69B2 2A B2 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_source)
319+  69B5 ED 5B B4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
320+  69B9              .bootstrap_block_loop:
321+  69B9 7A                   ld      a, d
322+  69BA B3                   or      e
323+  69BB 28 0B                jr      z, .bootstrap_block_done
324+  69BD 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
325+  69BE CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
326+  69C1 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
327+  69C4 23                   inc     hl
328+  69C5 1B                   dec     de
329+  69C6 18 F1                jr      .bootstrap_block_loop
330+  69C8              .bootstrap_block_done:
331+  69C8
332+  69C8                      ; Phase 5: #13 JUMP TO ADDRESS. The low byte is the command's
333+  69C8                      ; first argument and must be written before issuing the command.
334+  69C8 3E 05                ld      a, 5
335+  69CA 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
336+  69CD AF                   xor     a                       ; entry address low = $00
337+  69CE CD EC 6A             call    gs_boot_write_command_argument
338+  69D1 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
339+  69D4 3E 13                ld      a, GS_CMD_JUMP_ADDRESS
340+  69D6 CD CF 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command
341+  69D9 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
342+  69DC 3E 40                ld      a, #40                  ; entry address high = $40
343+  69DE CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
344+  69E1 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
345+  69E4
346+  69E4                      ; Phase 6: bootstrap has enabled RAM-over-ROM and is waiting at $0000.
347+  69E4 3E 06                ld      a, 6
348+  69E6 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
349+  69E9 CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
350+  69EC DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
351+  69EF FE A1                cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_READY_ACK
352+  69F1 C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
353+  69F4
354+  69F4                      ; Phase 7: send firmware length low/high and require ACK #A3.
355+  69F4 3E 07                ld      a, 7
356+  69F6 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
357+  69F9 2A B8 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_length)
358+  69FC 7D                   ld      a, l
359+  69FD CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
360+  6A00 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
361+  6A03 7C                   ld      a, h
362+  6A04 CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
363+  6A07 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
364+  6A0A CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
365+  6A0D DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
366+  6A10 FE A3                cp      NGS_BOOTSTRAP_LENGTH_ACK
367+  6A12 C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
368+  6A15
369+  6A15                      ; Phase 8: stream the custom firmware to RAM address $0000.
370+  6A15 3E 08                ld      a, 8
371+  6A17 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
372+  6A1A 2A B6 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
373+  6A1D ED 5B B8 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_length)
374+  6A21              .firmware_stream_loop:
375+  6A21 7A                   ld      a, d
376+  6A22 B3                   or      e
377+  6A23 28 31                jr      z, .firmware_stream_done
378+  6A25
379+  6A25                      ; Refill the same 256-byte host buffer only after its current bytes
380+  6A25                      ; have all been consumed. DE (firmware bytes left) survives the read.
381+  6A25 ED 4B A7 6B          ld      bc, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
382+  6A29 78                   ld      a, b
383+  6A2A B1                   or      c
384+  6A2B 20 11                jr      nz, .firmware_buffer_ready
385+  6A2D D5                   push    de
386+  6A2E CD 6A 68             call    neo_fw_read_next_sector
387+  6A31 D1                   pop     de
388+  6A32 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
389+  6A35 21 00 01             ld      hl, 256
390+  6A38 22 A7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
391+  6A3B 21 00 C0             ld      hl, #C000
392+  6A3E              .firmware_buffer_ready:
393+  6A3E 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
394+  6A3F CD 09 6B             call    gs_boot_send_data_byte
395+  6A42 DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
396+  6A45 23                   inc     hl
397+  6A46 1B                   dec     de
398+  6A47 22 B6 6B             ld      (neo_fw_source), hl
399+  6A4A 2A A7 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_buffer_remaining)
400+  6A4D 2B                   dec     hl
401+  6A4E 22 A7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_buffer_remaining), hl
402+  6A51 2A B6 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_source)
403+  6A54 18 CB                jr      .firmware_stream_loop
404+  6A56
405+  6A56              .firmware_stream_done:
406+  6A56                      ; Phase 9: new firmware has jumped to $0000 and sends ACK #A2.
407+  6A56 3E 09                ld      a, 9
408+  6A58 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
409+  6A5B CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
410+  6A5E DA 83 6A             jp      c, .upload_failed
411+  6A61 FE A2                cp      NGS_FIRMWARE_READY_ACK
412+  6A63 C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
413+  6A66
414+  6A66                      ; Cross-check every GS data byte acknowledged by DBIT. The count
415+  6A66                      ; includes #14's remaining three header bytes, bootstrap payload,
416+  6A66                      ; #13's high address byte, firmware length (two bytes), and firmware.
417+  6A66                      ; The first argument byte of #14 and #13 is preloaded before command
418+  6A66                      ; dispatch and is therefore intentionally not counted.
419+  6A66 3E 0A                ld      a, 10
420+  6A68 32 C8 6B             ld      (neo_fw_timeout_phase), a
421+  6A6B 2A B4 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bootstrap_length)
422+  6A6E ED 5B B8 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_length)
423+  6A72 19                   add     hl, de
424+  6A73 11 06 00             ld      de, 6
425+  6A76 19                   add     hl, de
426+  6A77 ED 5B C4 6B          ld      de, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
427+  6A7B B7                   or      a
428+  6A7C ED 52                sbc     hl, de
429+  6A7E C2 83 6A             jp      nz, .upload_failed
430+  6A81
431+  6A81 AF                   xor     a
432+  6A82 C9                   ret
433+  6A83
434+  6A83              .upload_failed:
435+  6A83 37                   scf
436+  6A84 C9                   ret
437+  6A85
438+  6A85              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
439+  6A85              ; Standard GS105a ROM transport primitives.
440+  6A85              ; Commands use CBIT; data bytes use DBIT. All waits are bounded.
441+  6A85              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
442+  6A85
443+  6A85              ; Probe stock ROM by reading the three-byte RAM size returned by command #20.
444+  6A85              gs_boot_probe_rom:
445+  6A85 3E 20                ld      a, GS_CMD_GET_TOTAL_RAM
446+  6A87 CD AA 6A             call    gs_boot_send_command_long
447+  6A8A D8                   ret     c
448+  6A8B CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
449+  6A8E D8                   ret     c
450+  6A8F 32 BE 6B             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_low), a
451+  6A92 CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
452+  6A95 D8                   ret     c
453+  6A96 32 BF 6B             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_mid), a
454+  6A99 CD 42 6B             call    gs_boot_receive_data_byte
455+  6A9C D8                   ret     c
456+  6A9D 32 C0 6B             ld      (neo_fw_rom_ram_high), a
457+  6AA0                      ; #20 reports total RAM as a 24-bit byte count. The 48-KiB
458+  6AA0                      ; memory map needs at least four 16-KiB pages (64 KiB total):
459+  6AA0                      ; fixed pages 0 and 3 plus selectable pages 1 and 2.
460+  6AA0 3A C0 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_rom_ram_high)
461+  6AA3 B7                   or      a
462+  6AA4 20 02                jr      nz, .rom_probe_ok
463+  6AA6 37                   scf
464+  6AA7 C9                   ret
465+  6AA8              .rom_probe_ok:
466+  6AA8 B7                   or      a
467+  6AA9 C9                   ret
468+  6AAA
469+  6AAA              ; Long command wait for #F4 cold restart and ROM self-test.
470+  6AAA              gs_boot_send_command_long:
471+  6AAA 32 C1 6B             ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
472+  6AAD 3A C1 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
473+  6AB0 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
474+  6AB2 D5                   push    de
475+  6AB3 16 10                ld      d, 16
476+  6AB5              .long_outer:
477+  6AB5 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
478+  6AB8              .long_inner:
479+  6AB8 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
480+  6ABA 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
481+  6ABD CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
482+  6ABF 28 0B                jr      z, .long_done
483+  6AC1 0B                   dec     bc
484+  6AC2 78                   ld      a, b
485+  6AC3 B1                   or      c
486+  6AC4 20 F2                jr      nz, .long_inner
487+  6AC6 15                   dec     d
488+  6AC7 20 EC                jr      nz, .long_outer
489+  6AC9 D1                   pop     de
490+  6ACA 37                   scf
491+  6ACB C9                   ret
492+  6ACC              .long_done:
493+  6ACC D1                   pop     de
494+  6ACD B7                   or      a
495+  6ACE C9                   ret
496+  6ACF
497+  6ACF              gs_boot_send_command:
498+  6ACF 32 C1 6B             ld      (neo_fw_command_byte), a
499+  6AD2 3A C1 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_command_byte)
500+  6AD5 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
501+  6AD7 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
502+  6ADA              .command_wait:
503+  6ADA DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
504+  6ADC 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
505+  6ADF CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
506+  6AE1 28 07                jr      z, .command_done
507+  6AE3 0B                   dec     bc
508+  6AE4 78                   ld      a, b
509+  6AE5 B1                   or      c
510+  6AE6 20 F2                jr      nz, .command_wait
511+  6AE8 37                   scf
512+  6AE9 C9                   ret
513+  6AEA              .command_done:
514+  6AEA B7                   or      a
515+  6AEB C9                   ret
516+  6AEC
517+  6AEC              ; Preload the first argument byte of GS105a commands #13/#14. Those
518+  6AEC              ; handlers read this byte before clearing CBIT; later arguments use DBIT.
519+  6AEC              gs_boot_write_command_argument:
520+  6AEC 32 C2 6B             ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
521+  6AEF 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
522+  6AF2              .argument_wait_free:
523+  6AF2 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
524+  6AF4 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
525+  6AF7 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
526+  6AF9 28 07                jr      z, .argument_slot_free
527+  6AFB 0B                   dec     bc
528+  6AFC 78                   ld      a, b
529+  6AFD B1                   or      c
530+  6AFE 20 F2                jr      nz, .argument_wait_free
531+  6B00 37                   scf
532+  6B01 C9                   ret
533+  6B02              .argument_slot_free:
534+  6B02 3A C2 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
535+  6B05 D3 B3                out     (GSDAT), a
536+  6B07 B7                   or      a
537+  6B08 C9                   ret
538+  6B09
539+  6B09              gs_boot_send_data_byte:
540+  6B09 32 C2 6B             ld      (neo_fw_tx_byte), a
541+  6B0C 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
542+  6B0F              .data_wait_free:
543+  6B0F DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
544+  6B11 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
545+  6B14 CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
546+  6B16 28 07                jr      z, .data_slot_free
547+  6B18 0B                   dec     bc
548+  6B19 78                   ld      a, b
549+  6B1A B1                   or      c
550+  6B1B 20 F2                jr      nz, .data_wait_free
551+  6B1D 37                   scf
552+  6B1E C9                   ret
553+  6B1F              .data_slot_free:
554+  6B1F 3A C2 6B             ld      a, (neo_fw_tx_byte)
555+  6B22 D3 B3                out     (GSDAT), a
556+  6B24 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
557+  6B27              .data_wait_consumed:
558+  6B27 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
559+  6B29 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
560+  6B2C CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
561+  6B2E 28 07                jr      z, .data_consumed
562+  6B30 0B                   dec     bc
563+  6B31 78                   ld      a, b
564+  6B32 B1                   or      c
565+  6B33 20 F2                jr      nz, .data_wait_consumed
566+  6B35 37                   scf
567+  6B36 C9                   ret
568+  6B37              .data_consumed:
569+  6B37 E5                   push    hl
570+  6B38 2A C4 6B             ld      hl, (neo_fw_bytes_sent)
571+  6B3B 23                   inc     hl
572+  6B3C 22 C4 6B             ld      (neo_fw_bytes_sent), hl
573+  6B3F E1                   pop     hl
574+  6B40 B7                   or      a
575+  6B41 C9                   ret
576+  6B42
577+  6B42              gs_boot_receive_data_byte:
578+  6B42 01 FF FF             ld      bc, FW_BYTE_TIMEOUT
579+  6B45              .receive_wait:
580+  6B45 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
581+  6B47 32 C7 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_status), a
582+  6B4A CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
583+  6B4C 20 07                jr      nz, .receive_ready
584+  6B4E 0B                   dec     bc
585+  6B4F 78                   ld      a, b
586+  6B50 B1                   or      c
587+  6B51 20 F2                jr      nz, .receive_wait
588+  6B53 37                   scf
589+  6B54 C9                   ret
590+  6B55              .receive_ready:
591+  6B55 DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
592+  6B57 32 C3 6B             ld      (neo_fw_last_rx_byte), a
593+  6B5A B7                   or      a
594+  6B5B C9                   ret
595+  6B5C
596+  6B5C              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
597+  6B5C              ; Project 124 private-protocol waits (custom firmware already running).
598+  6B5C              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
599+  6B5C              neogs_wait_command_done:
599+  6B5C
600+  6B5C 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
601+  6B5F              .cmd_wait:
602+  6B5F DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
603+  6B61 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
604+  6B63 28 07                jr      z, .cmd_done
605+  6B65 0B                   dec     bc
606+  6B66 78                   ld      a, b
607+  6B67 B1                   or      c
608+  6B68 20 F5                jr      nz, .cmd_wait
609+  6B6A 37                   scf
610+  6B6B C9                   ret
611+  6B6C              .cmd_done:

613+  6B6D C9                   ret

619+  6B71 01 FF FF             ld      bc, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
620+  6B74              .cmd_long_inner:

622+  6B76 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
623+  6B78 28 0B                jr      z, .cmd_long_done

625+  6B7B 78                   ld      a, b
626+  6B7C B1                   or      c

630+  6B82 D1                   pop     de

632+  6B84 C9                   ret

# file opened: x86_loader.asm
  1+  6BC9              ; =========================================================================
  2+  6BC9              ; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS task loader + NeoGS DMA / IPC
  3+  6BC9              ; Hardware protocol is based on NeoGS Programmer's Guide v0.1.A and
  4+  6BC9              ; ports.inc v0.3. The application-level command IDs/frame are Project 124.
  5+  6BC9              ;
  6+  6BC9              ; DMA mapping used here (proven working configuration):
  7+  6BC9              ;   NeoGS physical DMA destination = $00D000
  8+  6BC9              ;   NeoGS Z80 logical DMA frame address = $5000 (default WIN1 page #03)
  9+  6BC9              ;   two-byte length header at $5000; x86 execution begins at $5002.
 10+  6BC9              ;
 11+  6BC9              ; Host DMA writes to Spectrum address $0000-$3FFF. For DMA WRITE there is
 12+  6BC9              ; NO dummy write. The dummy access described by the user is only for DMA READ.
 13+  6BC9              ; =========================================================================
 14+  6BC9
 15+  6BC9              ; neogs_io.inc is included once by neogs_driver.asm before this module.
 16+  6BC9
 17+  6BC9              DMA_HOST_WINDOW    EQU #0000
 18+  6BC9              DMA_WINDOW_LIMIT   EQU #3000
 19+  6BC9

 24+  6BD3 31 FF BF             ld      sp, #BFFF              ; buffer starts at #C000; stack below it

221+  6E11
222+  6E11                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
223+  6E11                      ; Stage 5A: search TR-DOS directory entry.
224+  6E11                      ; Name #5CDD..#5CE4, type #5CE5, per the working project path.
225+  6E11                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
226+  6E11 2A 31 7D             ld      hl, (P124_X86_REQUEST_FILENAME)
227+  6E14 11 DD 5C             ld      de, #5CDD
228+  6E17 01 08 00             ld      bc, 8
229+  6E1A ED B0                ldir
230+  6E1C 3A 33 7D             ld      a, (P124_X86_REQUEST_TYPE)
231+  6E1F 32 E5 5C             ld      (#5CE5), a
232+  6E22 3E 09                ld      a, 9
233+  6E24 32 06 5D             ld      (#5D06), a
234+  6E27 0E 0A                ld      c, 10
235+  6E29 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
236+  6E2C 79                   ld      a, c
237+  6E2D FE FF                cp      #FF
238+  6E2F CA 3B 71             jp      z, .file_not_found
239+  6E32
240+  6E32                      ; C=#0A returns only the catalogue entry number. Fetch the full
241+  6E32                      ; 16-byte descriptor explicitly with C=#08 before using #5CE8..#5CEC.
242+  6E32 79                   ld      a, c
243+  6E33 F5                   push    af
244+  6E34 AF                   xor     a
245+  6E35 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
246+  6E38 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
247+  6E3B F1                   pop     af
248+  6E3C 0E 08                ld      c, 8
249+  6E3E CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
250+  6E41 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
251+  6E44 47                   ld      b, a
252+  6E45 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
253+  6E48 B0                   or      b
254+  6E49 C2 53 71             jp      nz, .bad_file_descriptor
255+  6E4C
256+  6E4C                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_file_ok
256+  6E4C             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
256+  6E4C E5          >                    PUSH    HL
256+  6E4D 21 74 7F    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_file_ok
256+  6E50 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
256+  6E53 E1          >                    POP     HL
256+  6E54             >                ENDIF
257+  6E54
258+  6E54                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
259+  6E54                      ; Stage 5B: validate descriptor BEFORE reading any sector.
260+  6E54                      ; #5CE8 = byte length, #5CEA = sector count,
261+  6E54                      ; #5CEB = first internal sector (0..15), #5CEC = track.
262+  6E54                      ; The DMA frame window is $5000..$7FFF (12 KiB). Its first two
263+  6E54                      ; bytes are the length header, so executable payload max is $2FFE.
264+  6E54                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
265+  6E54 ED 4B E8 5C          ld      bc, (#5CE8)
266+  6E58 78                   ld      a, b
267+  6E59 B1                   or      c
268+  6E5A CA 47 71             jp      z, .empty_file_error
269+  6E5D ED 43 34 7D          ld      (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH), bc
270+  6E61
271+  6E61                      ; Reject files larger than the executable 12 KB window minus header.
272+  6E61 78                   ld      a, b
273+  6E62 FE 2F                cp      #2F
274+  6E64 38 09                jr      c, .file_length_ok
275+  6E66 C2 80 71             jp      nz, .task_too_large
276+  6E69 79                   ld      a, c
277+  6E6A FE FF                cp      #FF
278+  6E6C D2 80 71             jp      nc, .task_too_large
279+  6E6F              .file_length_ok:
280+  6E6F                      ; Sector count must equal ceil(file_length / 256).
281+  6E6F 2A 34 7D             ld      hl, (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH)
282+  6E72 11 FF 00             ld      de, 255
283+  6E75 19                   add     hl, de
284+  6E76 5C                   ld      e, h
285+  6E77 3A EA 5C             ld      a, (#5CEA)
286+  6E7A B7                   or      a
287+  6E7B CA 47 71             jp      z, .empty_file_error
288+  6E7E BB                   cp      e
289+  6E7F C2 53 71             jp      nz, .bad_file_descriptor
290+  6E82 32 38 7D             ld      (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING), a
291+  6E85
292+  6E85 3A EB 5C             ld      a, (#5CEB)
293+  6E88 FE 10                cp      16
294+  6E8A D2 53 71             jp      nc, .bad_file_descriptor
295+  6E8D 32 3A 7D             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
296+  6E90 3A EC 5C             ld      a, (#5CEC)
297+  6E93 32 39 7D             ld      (P124_X86_READ_TRACK), a
298+  6E96 21 00 C0             ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
299+  6E99 22 36 7D             ld      (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER), hl
300+  6E9C
301+  6E9C              .read_sector_loop:
302+  6E9C                      ; Read exactly one sector per TR-DOS #05 call.
303+  6E9C 3A 39 7D             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_TRACK)
304+  6E9F 57                   ld      d, a
305+  6EA0 3A 3A 7D             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR)
306+  6EA3 5F                   ld      e, a
307+  6EA4 2A 36 7D             ld      hl, (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER)
308+  6EA7 06 01                ld      b, 1
309+  6EA9 0E 05                ld      c, 5
310+  6EAB AF                   xor     a
311+  6EAC 32 0F 5D             ld      (#5D0F), a
312+  6EAF 32 10 5D             ld      (#5D10), a
313+  6EB2 CD 13 3D             call    #3D13
314+  6EB5                      ; C=#05 stores its two-byte error result at #5D0F/#5D10.
315+  6EB5 3A 0F 5D             ld      a, (#5D0F)
316+  6EB8 47                   ld      b, a
317+  6EB9 3A 10 5D             ld      a, (#5D10)
318+  6EBC B0                   or      b
319+  6EBD C2 5F 71             jp      nz, .sector_read_error
320+  6EC0
321+  6EC0                      ; Advance host buffer by 256 bytes.
322+  6EC0 2A 36 7D             ld      hl, (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER)
323+  6EC3 11 00 01             ld      de, 256
324+  6EC6 19                   add     hl, de
325+  6EC7 22 36 7D             ld      (P124_X86_READ_BUFFER), hl
326+  6ECA
327+  6ECA                      ; TR-DOS sectors are numbered 0..15 in this project convention.
328+  6ECA 3A 3A 7D             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR)
329+  6ECD 3C                   inc     a
330+  6ECE FE 10                cp      16
331+  6ED0 38 0D                jr      c, .sector_same_track
332+  6ED2 AF                   xor     a
333+  6ED3 32 3A 7D             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
334+  6ED6 3A 39 7D             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_TRACK)
335+  6ED9 3C                   inc     a
336+  6EDA 32 39 7D             ld      (P124_X86_READ_TRACK), a
337+  6EDD 18 03                jr      .sector_advanced
338+  6EDF
339+  6EDF              .sector_same_track:
340+  6EDF 32 3A 7D             ld      (P124_X86_READ_SECTOR), a
341+  6EE2
342+  6EE2              .sector_advanced:
343+  6EE2 3A 38 7D             ld      a, (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING)
344+  6EE5 3D                   dec     a
345+  6EE6 32 38 7D             ld      (P124_X86_READ_REMAINING), a
346+  6EE9 C2 9C 6E             jp      nz, .read_sector_loop
347+  6EEC
348+  6EEC                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_buf_loaded
348+  6EEC             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
348+  6EEC E5          >                    PUSH    HL
348+  6EED 21 98 7F    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_buf_loaded
348+  6EF0 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
348+  6EF3 E1          >                    POP     HL
348+  6EF4             >                ENDIF
349+  6EF4
350+  6EF4                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
351+  6EF4                      ; Stage 5C: transfer actual task. The DMA frame contains a two-byte
352+  6EF4                      ; length prefix, therefore the total transfer must fit in $3000 bytes.
353+  6EF4                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
354+  6EF4 ED 4B 34 7D          ld      bc, (P124_X86_FILE_LENGTH)
355+  6EF8 21 00 C0             ld      hl, TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER
356+  6EFB CD B5 71             call    neogs_send_dma_block
357+  6EFE DA 8C 71             jp      c, .task_dma_error
358+  6F01                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_task_dma_ok
358+  6F01             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
358+  6F01 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
358+  6F02 21 D5 7F    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_task_dma_ok
358+  6F05 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
358+  6F08 E1          >                    POP     HL
358+  6F09             >                ENDIF
359+  6F09
360+  6F09                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
361+  6F09                      ; Stage 5D: execute and fetch framed result.
362+  6F09                      ; -------------------------------------------------------------
363+  6F09 CD 1C 72             call    neogs_run_x86_real_and_get_result
364+  6F0C DA 98 71             jp      c, .result_timeout
365+  6F0F
366+  6F0F                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_x86_result_ready
366+  6F0F             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
366+  6F0F E5          >                    PUSH    HL
366+  6F10 21 09 80    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_x86_result_ready
366+  6F13 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
366+  6F16 E1          >                    POP     HL
366+  6F17             >                ENDIF
367+  6F17 3A 3C 7D             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
368+  6F1A 6F                   ld      l, a
369+  6F1B 26 00                ld      h, 0
370+  6F1D                      MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
370+  6F1D             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
370+  6F1D E5          >                    PUSH    HL
370+  6F1E E5          >                    PUSH    hl
370+  6F1F E1          >                    POP     HL
370+  6F20 CD 81 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintHex16
370+  6F23 E1          >                    POP     HL
370+  6F24             >                ENDIF
371+  6F24                      MACRO_EF_CHAR 32
371+  6F24             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
371+  6F24 F5          >                    PUSH    AF
371+  6F25 C5          >                    PUSH    BC
371+  6F26 3E 20       >                    LD      A, 32
371+  6F28 01 EF 00    >                    LD      BC, #00EF
371+  6F2B ED 79       >                    OUT     (C), A
371+  6F2D C1          >                    POP     BC
371+  6F2E F1          >                    POP     AF
371+  6F2F             >                ENDIF
372+  6F2F 2A 3D 7D             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
373+  6F32                      MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
373+  6F32             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
373+  6F32 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
373+  6F33 E5          >                    PUSH    hl
373+  6F34 E1          >                    POP     HL
373+  6F35 CD 81 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintHex16
373+  6F38 E1          >                    POP     HL
373+  6F39             >                ENDIF
374+  6F39                      MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
374+  6F39             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
374+  6F39 F5          >                    PUSH    AF
374+  6F3A C5          >                    PUSH    BC
374+  6F3B 3E 0D       >                    LD      A, 13
374+  6F3D 01 EF 00    >                    LD      BC, #00EF
374+  6F40 ED 79       >                    OUT     (C), A
374+  6F42 C1          >                    POP     BC
374+  6F43 F1          >                    POP     AF
374+  6F44             >                ENDIF
375+  6F44
376+  6F44 3A 3C 7D             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
377+  6F47 B7                   or      a
378+  6F48 C2 A4 71             jp      nz, .interpreter_error
379+  6F4B
380+  6F4B                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_stack_guard_pass
380+  6F4B             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
380+  6F4B E5          >                    PUSH    HL
380+  6F4C 21 2B 80    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_stack_guard_pass
380+  6F4F CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
380+  6F52 E1          >                    POP     HL
380+  6F53             >                ENDIF
381+  6F53 2A 3D 7D             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
382+  6F56 CD 69 8C             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintHex16
383+  6F59 3E 0D                ld      a, 13
384+  6F5B CD 76 8B             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
385+  6F5E 3E 0A                ld      a, 10
386+  6F60 CD 76 8B             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
387+  6F63 B7                   or      a
388+  6F64 C3 B0 71             jp      .loader_restore_stack
389+  6F67
390+  6F67              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
391+  6F67              ; Error exits. All return through the same original-stack restoration.
392+  6F67              ; -------------------------------------------------------------------------
393+  6F67              .diag_dma_error:
394+  6F67                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_dma_fail
394+  6F67             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
394+  6F67 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
394+  6F68 21 DE 7D    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_ipc_diag_dma_fail
394+  6F6B CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
394+  6F6E E1          >                    POP     HL
394+  6F6F             >                ENDIF
395+  6F6F 37                   scf
396+  6F70 C3 B0 71             jp      .loader_restore_stack
397+  6F73
398+  6F73              .diag_protocol_error:
399+  6F73                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_ipc_diag_frame_fail

535+  7166 E1          >                    POP     HL
535+  7167             >                ENDIF
536+  7167 2A 0F 5D             ld      hl, (#5D0F)
537+  716A                      MACRO_EF_HEX16 hl
537+  716A             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
537+  716A E5          >                    PUSH    HL
537+  716B E5          >                    PUSH    hl
537+  716C E1          >                    POP     HL
537+  716D CD 81 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintHex16
537+  7170 E1          >                    POP     HL
537+  7171             >                ENDIF
538+  7171                      MACRO_EF_CHAR 13
538+  7171             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
538+  7171 F5          >                    PUSH    AF
538+  7172 C5          >                    PUSH    BC
538+  7173 3E 0D       >                    LD      A, 13
538+  7175 01 EF 00    >                    LD      BC, #00EF
538+  7178 ED 79       >                    OUT     (C), A
538+  717A C1          >                    POP     BC
538+  717B F1          >                    POP     AF
538+  717C             >                ENDIF
539+  717C 37                   scf
540+  717D C3 B0 71             jp      .loader_restore_stack
541+  7180
542+  7180              .task_too_large:
543+  7180                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_err_task_too_large
543+  7180             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
543+  7180 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
543+  7181 21 42 81    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_err_task_too_large
543+  7184 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
543+  7187 E1          >                    POP     HL
543+  7188             >                ENDIF
544+  7188 37                   scf
545+  7189 C3 B0 71             jp      .loader_restore_stack
546+  718C
547+  718C              .task_dma_error:
548+  718C                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_err_dma_transfer
548+  718C             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
548+  718C E5          >                    PUSH    HL
548+  718D 21 93 81    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_err_dma_transfer
548+  7190 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
548+  7193 E1          >                    POP     HL
548+  7194             >                ENDIF
549+  7194 37                   scf
550+  7195 C3 B0 71             jp      .loader_restore_stack
551+  7198
552+  7198              .result_timeout:
553+  7198                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_err_result_timeout
553+  7198             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
553+  7198 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
553+  7199 21 C2 81    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_err_result_timeout
553+  719C CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
553+  719F E1          >                    POP     HL
553+  71A0             >                ENDIF
554+  71A0 37                   scf
555+  71A1 C3 B0 71             jp      .loader_restore_stack
556+  71A4
557+  71A4              .interpreter_error:
558+  71A4                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_err_interpreter_status
558+  71A4             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
558+  71A4 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
558+  71A5 21 F3 81    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_err_interpreter_status
558+  71A8 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
558+  71AB E1          >                    POP     HL
558+  71AC             >                ENDIF
559+  71AC 37                   scf
560+  71AD C3 B0 71             jp      .loader_restore_stack
561+  71B0
562+  71B0              .loader_restore_stack:
563+  71B0 2A 29 7D             ld      hl, (P124_X86_LOADER_SP_BACKUP)
564+  71B3 F9                   ld      sp, hl
565+  71B4 C9                   ret
566+  71B5
567+  71B5              ; =========================================================================
568+  71B5              ; NeoGS transport helpers — exact CBIT/DBIT meanings from Guide v0.1.A
569+  71B5              ; =========================================================================
570+  71B5
571+  71B5              ; IN: HL=source in host RAM, BC=payload byte count (1..#2FFE).
572+  71B5              ; OUT: Carry clear after NeoGS armed DMA, consumed the host end marker,
573+  71B5              ;      and disabled DMA. Carry set on timeout.
574+  71B5              ;
575+  71B5              ; Protocol:
576+  71B5              ;   host OUT GSCOM,CMD_LOAD_X86 -> CBIT=1
577+  71B5              ;   NeoGS configures DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (READY)
578+  71B5              ;   host LDIR to #0000..#3FFF (no dummy write)
579+  71B5              ;   host OUT GSCOM,#FF -> CBIT=1 (end marker)
580+  71B5              ;   NeoGS disables DMA, clears CLRCBIT -> CBIT=0 (DONE)
581+  71B5              neogs_send_dma_block:
582+  71B5 78                   ld      a, b
583+  71B6 B1                   or      c
584+  71B7 CA 0F 72             jp      z, .dma_bad_length
585+  71BA 78                   ld      a, b
586+  71BB FE 40                cp      #40
587+  71BD 38 08                jr      c, .dma_length_ok
588+  71BF C2 0F 72             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
589+  71C2 79                   ld      a, c
590+  71C3 B7                   or      a
591+  71C4 C2 0F 72             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
592+  71C7              .dma_length_ok:
593+  71C7 22 2B 7D             ld      (P124_DMA_SOURCE), hl
594+  71CA ED 43 2D 7D          ld      (P124_DMA_LENGTH), bc
595+  71CE
596+  71CE                      ; Validate payload + 2-byte length prefix <= $3000.
597+  71CE C5                   push    bc
598+  71CF E1                   pop     hl
599+  71D0 11 02 00             ld      de, 2
600+  71D3 19                   add     hl, de
601+  71D4 7C                   ld      a, h
602+  71D5 FE 30                cp      #30
603+  71D7 38 08                jr      c, .dma_total_length_ok
604+  71D9 C2 0F 72             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
605+  71DC 7D                   ld      a, l
606+  71DD B7                   or      a
607+  71DE C2 0F 72             jp      nz, .dma_bad_length
608+  71E1              .dma_total_length_ok:
609+  71E1 3E 02                ld      a, CMD_LOAD_X86
610+  71E3 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
611+  71E5 CD 5C 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done
612+  71E8 D8                   ret     c
613+  71E9
614+  71E9                      ; LDIR is used for BOTH pieces of the frame. This is intentional:
615+  71E9                      ; the DMA engine observes host memory-write cycles. The first LDIR
616+  71E9                      ; writes the two-byte header to host addresses #0000-#0001; the second
617+  71E9                      ; writes the payload to #0002 onward. No direct LD (nn),A writes and
618+  71E9                      ; no temporary stack are needed.
619+  71E9 F3                   di
620+  71EA 2A 2D 7D             ld      hl, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
621+  71ED 22 2F 7D             ld      (P124_DMA_HEADER), hl
622+  71F0 21 2F 7D             ld      hl, P124_DMA_HEADER
623+  71F3 11 00 00             ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW
624+  71F6 01 02 00             ld      bc, 2
625+  71F9 ED B0                ldir
626+  71FB
627+  71FB 2A 2B 7D             ld      hl, (P124_DMA_SOURCE)
628+  71FE 11 02 00             ld      de, DMA_HOST_WINDOW + 2
629+  7201 ED 4B 2D 7D          ld      bc, (P124_DMA_LENGTH)
630+  7205 ED B0                ldir
631+  7207
632+  7207                      ; End marker is a command-byte write; it is NOT a DMA data byte.
633+  7207 3E FF                ld      a, #FF
634+  7209 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
635+  720B CD 5C 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done
636+  720E C9                   ret
637+  720F
638+  720F              .dma_bad_length:
639+  720F 37                   scf
640+  7210 C9                   ret
641+  7211
642+  7211              ; Send RUN command and fetch the four-byte application result frame:
643+  7211              ; A5 signature, status, AX low, AX high.
644+  7211              neogs_run_and_get_result:
645+  7211 3E 03                ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86
646+  7213 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
647+  7215 CD 32 72             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
648+  7218 D8                   ret     c
649+  7219 C3 DA 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
650+  721C
651+  721C              ; Execute the actual TR-DOS task with the CS:IP real-mode core.
652+  721C              neogs_run_x86_real_and_get_result:
653+  721C 3E 0B                ld      a, CMD_RUN_X86_REAL
654+  721E D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
655+  7220 CD 32 72             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
656+  7223 D8                   ret     c
657+  7224 C3 DA 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
658+  7227
659+  7227              ; Stage 3A guest BIOS video test. Its output is drained while the NeoGS
660+  7227              ; command remains active; DBIT bytes are never confused with the result frame.
661+  7227              neogs_bios_video_test_and_get_result:
662+  7227 3E 0A                ld      a, CMD_BIOS_VIDEO_TEST
663+  7229 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
664+  722B CD 32 72             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
665+  722E D8                   ret     c
666+  722F C3 DA 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
667+  7232
668+  7232              ; Wait for the RUN command while servicing the bidirectional data channel.
669+  7232              ; Stream protocol: nonzero=character, 00 00=clear, 00 01 row col=set cursor.
670+  7232              ; Each character is sent to emulator printer port #EF and rendered on screen.
671+  7232              neogs_wait_run_done_with_console:
672+  7232 D5                   push    de
673+  7233 16 10                ld      d, #10
674+  7235              .run_outer:
675+  7235 21 FF FF             ld      hl, IPC_CMD_TIMEOUT
676+  7238              .run_inner:
677+  7238 DB BB                in      a, (GSSTAT)
678+  723A CB 7F                bit     B_DBIT, a
679+  723C 28 38                jr      z, .no_stream_byte
680+  723E DB B3                in      a, (GSDAT)
681+  7240 B7                   or      a
682+  7241 28 0C                jr      z, .stream_control
683+  7243 F5                   push    af
684+  7244 01 EF 00             ld      bc, #00EF
685+  7247 ED 79                out     (c), a
686+  7249 F1                   pop     af
687+  724A CD 76 8B             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar
688+  724D 18 E9                jr      .run_inner
689+  724F
690+  724F              .stream_control:
691+  724F CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
692+  7252 38 2E                jr      c, .run_timeout
693+  7254 B7                   or      a
694+  7255 28 1A                jr      z, .stream_clear
695+  7257 FE 01                cp      1
696+  7259 20 DD                jr      nz, .run_inner
697+  725B CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
698+  725E 38 22                jr      c, .run_timeout
699+  7260 32 42 7D             ld      (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW), a
700+  7263 CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
701+  7266 38 1A                jr      c, .run_timeout
702+  7268 4F                   ld      c, a
703+  7269 3A 42 7D             ld      a, (P124_BIOS_STREAM_ROW)
704+  726C CD 68 8B             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_SetCursor
705+  726F 18 C7                jr      .run_inner
706+  7271
707+  7271              .stream_clear:
708+  7271 CD 1D 8B             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_Clear
709+  7274 18 C2                jr      .run_inner
710+  7276
711+  7276              .no_stream_byte:
712+  7276 CB 47                bit     B_CBIT, a
713+  7278 28 0B                jr      z, .run_done
714+  727A 2B                   dec     hl
715+  727B 7C                   ld      a, h
716+  727C B5                   or      l
717+  727D 20 B9                jr      nz, .run_inner
718+  727F 15                   dec     d
719+  7280 20 B3                jr      nz, .run_outer
720+  7282              .run_timeout:
721+  7282 D1                   pop     de
722+  7283 37                   scf
723+  7284 C9                   ret
724+  7285              .run_done:
725+  7285 D1                   pop     de
726+  7286 B7                   or      a
727+  7287 C9                   ret
728+  7288
729+  7288              ; Run the BIOS low-memory POST and fetch the existing four-byte result frame.
730+  7288              neogs_bios_post_and_get_result:
731+  7288 3E 07                ld      a, CMD_BIOS_POST
732+  728A D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
733+  728C CD 6E 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
734+  728F D8                   ret     c
735+  7290 C3 DA 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
736+  7293
737+  7293              ; Run the 8086 interrupt entry/return architectural self-test.
738+  7293              neogs_int_test_and_get_result:
739+  7293 3E 08                ld      a, CMD_INT_TEST
740+  7295 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
741+  7297 CD 6E 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
742+  729A D8                   ret     c
743+  729B C3 DA 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
744+  729E
745+  729E              ; Run a real guest INT/IRET handler test and fetch the result frame.
746+  729E              neogs_int_guest_test_and_get_result:
747+  729E 3E 09                ld      a, CMD_INT_GUEST_TEST
748+  72A0 D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
749+  72A2                      ; Drain NeoGS trace characters over DBIT while guest regression runs.
750+  72A2                      ; The same host routine mirrors every byte to emulator port #EF.
751+  72A2 CD 32 72             call    neogs_wait_run_done_with_console
752+  72A5 D8                   ret     c
753+  72A6 C3 DA 72             jp      neogs_get_result_frame
754+  72A9
755+  72A9              ; Run the NeoGS physical-memory self-test and fetch its result frame.
756+  72A9              neogs_mem_test_and_get_result:
757+  72A9 3E 05                ld      a, CMD_MEM_TEST
758+  72AB D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
759+  72AD                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_command_sent
759+  72AD             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
759+  72AD E5          >                    PUSH    HL
759+  72AE 21 41 73    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_memtest_command_sent
759+  72B1 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
759+  72B4 E1          >                    POP     HL
759+  72B5             >                ENDIF
760+  72B5 CD 6E 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done_long
761+  72B8 30 0A                jr      nc, .memtest_command_ack
762+  72BA                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_command_timeout
762+  72BA             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
762+  72BA E5          >                    PUSH    HL
762+  72BB 21 B4 73    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_memtest_command_timeout
762+  72BE CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
762+  72C1 E1          >                    POP     HL
762+  72C2             >                ENDIF
763+  72C2 37                   scf
764+  72C3 C9                   ret
765+  72C4              .memtest_command_ack:
766+  72C4                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_command_ack
766+  72C4             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
766+  72C4 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
766+  72C5 21 7B 73    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_memtest_command_ack
766+  72C8 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
766+  72CB E1          >                    POP     HL
766+  72CC             >                ENDIF
767+  72CC CD DA 72             call    neogs_get_result_frame
768+  72CF D0                   ret     nc
769+  72D0                      MACRO_EF_STRING P124_msg_memtest_result_timeout
769+  72D0             >                IF ENABLE_HARDWARE_DEBUG == 1
769+  72D0 E5          >                    PUSH    HL
769+  72D1 21 FA 73    >                    LD      HL, P124_msg_memtest_result_timeout
769+  72D4 CD 6B 60    >                    CALL    DBG_Port_PrintString
769+  72D7 E1          >                    POP     HL
769+  72D8             >                ENDIF
770+  72D8 37                   scf
771+  72D9 C9                   ret
772+  72DA
773+  72DA              neogs_get_result_frame:
774+  72DA 3E 04                ld      a, CMD_GET_STATUS
775+  72DC D3 BB                out     (GSCOM), a
776+  72DE
777+  72DE CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
778+  72E1 D8                   ret     c
779+  72E2 32 3B 7D             ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_MAGIC), a
780+  72E5
781+  72E5 CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
782+  72E8 D8                   ret     c
783+  72E9 32 3C 7D             ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS), a
784+  72EC
785+  72EC CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
786+  72EF D8                   ret     c
787+  72F0 5F                   ld      e, a
788+  72F1
789+  72F1 CD 88 6B             call    neogs_wait_data_byte
790+  72F4 D8                   ret     c
791+  72F5 57                   ld      d, a
792+  72F6 ED 53 3D 7D          ld      (P124_X86_RESULT_AX), de
793+  72FA ED 53 03 65          ld      (X86_RESULT_AX), de
794+  72FE
795+  72FE CD 5C 6B             call    neogs_wait_command_done
796+  7301 D8                   ret     c
797+  7302
798+  7302 3A 3B 7D             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_MAGIC)
799+  7305 FE A5                cp      NGS_RESULT_SIGNATURE
800+  7307 28 02                jr      z, .result_frame_ok
801+  7309 37                   scf
802+  730A C9                   ret

804+  730B B7                   or      a
805+  730C C9                   ret

853+  7CF5              x86_diag_inc_wrap:

867+  7D00              x86_diag_fallthrough_end:
868+  7D00
869+  7D00              ; Run one diagnostic payload through DMA, execute it, and compare both
870+  7D00              ; interpreter status and AX. IN: HL=payload, BC=length, A=expected status,
871+  7D00              ; DE=expected AX. OUT: carry clear on exact match, set on any transport/result
872+  7D00              ; mismatch. This deliberately accepts the expected error statuses above.
873+  7D00              neogs_run_diag_case:
874+  7D00 32 3F 7D             ld      (P124_DIAG_EXPECT_STATUS), a
875+  7D03 ED 53 40 7D          ld      (P124_DIAG_EXPECT_AX), de
876+  7D07 CD B5 71             call    neogs_send_dma_block
877+  7D0A D8                   ret     c

880+  7D0F 3A 3C 7D             ld      a, (P124_X86_RESULT_STATUS)
881+  7D12 47                   ld      b, a
882+  7D13 3A 3F 7D             ld      a, (P124_DIAG_EXPECT_STATUS)
883+  7D16 B8                   cp      b
884+  7D17 20 0E                jr      nz, .diag_case_failed
885+  7D19 2A 3D 7D             ld      hl, (P124_X86_RESULT_AX)
886+  7D1C ED 5B 40 7D          ld      de, (P124_DIAG_EXPECT_AX)
887+  7D20 B7                   or      a
888+  7D21 ED 52                sbc     hl, de
889+  7D23 20 02                jr      nz, .diag_case_failed
890+  7D25 B7                   or      a
891+  7D26 C9                   ret
892+  7D27              .diag_case_failed:
893+  7D27 37                   scf
894+  7D28 C9                   ret

  1+  8B00              ; =========================================================================
  2+  8B00              ; PROJECT 124 — BIOS 6x8 debug console backend
  3+  8B00              ; Lower Spectrum window: 42 columns x 12 rows, 6 pixels x 8 scanlines.
  4+  8B00              ; Uses font68.bin (96 glyphs, 8 bytes per glyph). This renderer is separate
  5+  8B00              ; from Direct_PrintChar so existing 32-column Commander panels are unchanged.
  6+  8B00              ; =========================================================================
  7+  8B00
  8+  8B00              BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS EQU 42
  9+  8B00              BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS    EQU 12
 10+  8B00              BIOS_DEBUG_TOP     EQU 12
 11+  8B00
 12+  8B00              BIOS_DebugConsole_Init:
 12+  8B00
 13+  8B00 3A F7 64             ld      a, (Console_Y)
 14+  8B03 D6 0C                sub     BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
 15+  8B05 30 01                jr      nc, .row_ok
 16+  8B07 AF                   xor     a
 17+  8B08              .row_ok:
 18+  8B08 FE 0C                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS
 19+  8B0A 38 02                jr      c, .row_valid
 20+  8B0C 3E 0B                ld      a, BIOS_DEBUG_ROWS - 1
 21+  8B0E              .row_valid:
 22+  8B0E 32 B8 8C             ld      (bios_dbg_row), a
 23+  8B11 3A F6 64             ld      a, (Console_X)
 24+  8B14 FE 2A                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS
 25+  8B16 38 01                jr      c, .col_valid
 26+  8B18 AF                   xor     a
 27+  8B19              .col_valid:
 28+  8B19 32 B9 8C             ld      (bios_dbg_col), a
 29+  8B1C C9                   ret
 30+  8B1D
 31+  8B1D              ; Clear only the lower 96-pixel-high window, not the Commander file panel.
 32+  8B1D              BIOS_DebugConsole_Clear:
 32+  8B1D
 33+  8B1D F5                   push    af
 34+  8B1E C5                   push    bc
 35+  8B1F D5                   push    de
 36+  8B20 E5                   push    hl

 39+  8B26              .clear_scanline:

  1+  8D67              ; =========================================================================
  2+  8D67              ; FILE: zx_main/file_utils.asm
  3+  8D67              ; PROJECT 124 — TR-DOS directory utilities
  4+  8D67              ; Sector API verified for TR-DOS 5.04T: C=#05, D=track, E=internal sector
  5+  8D67              ; 0..15, B=count, HL=destination; error status at #5D0F.
  6+  8D67              ; =========================================================================
  7+  8D67
  8+  8D67              ; IN: CursorY = visible row (2..10)
  9+  8D67              ; OUT: HL = 16-byte descriptor in TRDOS_PANEL_BUFFER, carry clear.
 10+  8D67              ;      HL=0 and carry set for an invalid/empty row.
 11+  8D67              GetSelectedFileDescriptor:

310   E195
311   E195              ; NeoGS firmware is built as a separate binary by neogs_sub/build_firmware.asm.
312   E195
313   E195              ; =========================================================================
314   E195              ; СЕКТОР ФИНАЛЬНОГО ОКОНЧАТЕЛЬНОГО ЭКСПОРТА НА ДИСК
315   E195              ; =========================================================================
316   E195                      PAGE    0                       ; Возвращаем базовый нулевой банк памяти хоста
317   E195
318   E195                      ; Синхронный экспорт бинарных блоков в образ TRD
319   E195                      SAVETRD "disk_A.trd", "proj124.C", 24576, ProgramEnd - 24576
320   E195                      SAVETRD "disk_A.trd", "boot.B", BASIC_START, BASIC_END - BASIC_START
321   E195                      ; One logical neoprog.C package, appended in 16-KiB chunks from
322   E195                      ; separate Spectrum RAM banks. Chunk boundaries are sector-aligned.
323   E195                      ASSERT NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT >= 1
324   E195                      ASSERT NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT <= 4
325   E195                      ASSERT NEOGS_PACKAGE_TOTAL_SIZE <= #FF00
326   E195
327   E195                      PAGE    1
328   E195                      ORG     #C000
329   C000              neoprog_chunk0_start:
330   C000                      INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_part0.bin"
331   FF4C              neoprog_chunk0_end:
332   FF4C                      ASSERT neoprog_chunk0_end - neoprog_chunk0_start = NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK0_SIZE
333   FF4C                      SAVETRD "disk_A.trd", "neoprog.C", #C000, NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK0_SIZE
334   FF4C
335   FF4C                      IF NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT > 1
336   FF4C ~                    PAGE    3
337   FF4C ~                    ORG     #C000
338   FF4C ~            neoprog_chunk1_start:
339   FF4C ~                    INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_part1.bin"
340   FF4C ~            neoprog_chunk1_end:
341   FF4C ~                    ASSERT neoprog_chunk1_end - neoprog_chunk1_start = NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK1_SIZE
342   FF4C ~                    SAVETRD "disk_A.trd",&"neoprog.C", #C000, NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK1_SIZE
343   FF4C                      ENDIF
344   FF4C
345   FF4C                      IF NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT > 2
346   FF4C ~                    PAGE    4
347   FF4C ~                    ORG     #C000
348   FF4C ~            neoprog_chunk2_start:
349   FF4C ~                    INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_part2.bin"
350   FF4C ~            neoprog_chunk2_end:
351   FF4C ~                    ASSERT neoprog_chunk2_end - neoprog_chunk2_start = NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK2_SIZE
352   FF4C ~                    SAVETRD "disk_A.trd",&"neoprog.C", #C000, NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK2_SIZE
353   FF4C                      ENDIF
354   FF4C
355   FF4C                      IF NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK_COUNT > 3
356   FF4C ~                    PAGE    6
357   FF4C ~                    ORG     #C000
358   FF4C ~            neoprog_chunk3_start:
359   FF4C ~                    INCBIN "zx_main/neoprog_part3.bin"
360   FF4C ~            neoprog_chunk3_end:
361   FF4C ~                    ASSERT neoprog_chunk3_end - neoprog_chunk3_start = NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK3_SIZE
362   FF4C ~                    SAVETRD "disk_A.trd",&"neoprog.C", #C000, NEOGS_PACKAGE_CHUNK3_SIZE
363   FF4C                      ENDIF
364   FF4C
365   FF4C                      PAGE    0
366   FF4C                      ; Separate x86 diagnostic task; never reuse the NeoGS package filename.
367   FF4C                      SAVETRD "disk_A.trd", "x86task.C", x86_diag_program, x86_diag_program_end - x86_diag_program
368   FF4C
369   FF4C
370   FF4C              ; =========================================================================
371   FF4C
# file closed: main.asm
